# Re-verification queue: rows at parity whose webview source changed upstream

Rows marked done after the reconciliation of parity.json with the merge of v0.1.46, compared between the fork's base `37c2a604` and `v0.1.46` with `git diff -M --numstat`. Done rows: **1487** (1413 at parity `[x]`, 74 model only `[m]`). Source changed: **1254** (1209 `[x]`, 45 `[m]`). Source unchanged: **233**.

Of the 408 done `unit:` rows, 97 have a spec whose case names changed upstream (cases added, removed or retitled) and still pass the generator's count check, because their proofs cite whole test modules or say after a semicolon why fewer tests suffice; 25 have the same case names with changed bodies; 169 have an unchanged spec and a changed implementation file.

Of the 325 done `ipc:` rows, 300 have an adapter or `ops` file that changed; in 37 of them the adapter function's or the called `ops::` function's own body changed; 110 adapters call no `ops::` function, so their backend was not looked at (they are in the table when their adapter file changed, and listed after it when it did not).

How a row's source is taken: an action, extension point, settings page or context key: the file the inventory names (line numbers dropped, so any change in the file counts); a module: its file or, for an editor group, its folder; a feature: its folder; a `unit:` row: its spec file plus the non-test files it imports (relative and `@/` imports, type-only imports left out); an `ipc:` row: the adapter's file in `src-tauri/src/commands/` and the file of each `ops::` function its adapter function calls in `crates/ide-host/src/ops/`, by whole-file numstat; the note also says whether the adapter function's body and each `ops::` function's body changed text to text (a body that compares equal can still call code that changed, so that is not the verdict), and an adapter with no `ops::` call (desktop shell) is judged on its own file with its backend not resolved; an event row: `crates/ide-core/src/event.rs` as a whole. Lines are added/removed summed over the row's files. Functions called further down are not followed.
